# Parity ledger: the desktop redesign

This ledger is the contract for the desktop redesign (#357): every capability the desktop app has before the redesign,
numbered, so that after the rebuild each one can be walked and ticked. A capability is anything a user, the operating
system or another program can observe: a control, a menu item, a shortcut, a setting, a state, a notification, a sound,
a window behaviour, a persisted preference, a deep link. If something the app does today is missing here, it can be lost
without anyone noticing: add it, with the next free ID.

It describes `dev` at `c6e514a` (after #355, "Draw every view with Duvet's palette and shapes"). It was written from the
code and its tests, file by file, not from the running app.

### How to read it

- **ID:** `P-001` onwards, sequential through the whole document. An ID is never reused: an item that turns out wrong is
  struck through, not deleted, and an item added later takes the next free number.
- **Capability:** what a user would recognise, in the app's words: a Space has rooms; DMs and groups are conversations.
- **Where:** the files and symbols that implement it. UI paths are relative to `src/mainview/src/`
  (`lib/components/Chat.svelte`); main-process paths start with `src/bun/`; `static/…`, `svelte.config.js` and
  `vite.config.ts` are in `src/mainview/`.
- **Platforms:** `all` is the web and the desktop app on macOS, Windows and Linux. Anything behind
  `isElectrobunWindow()`, an Electrobun RPC or a platform check names its platforms.
- **How to check:** steps in the running app, and what to expect. Checks need the local stack (`redesign-work/STACK.md`),
  never production, and say what else they need (a second account, a Space where you manage roles, …).
- **Tests:** the test files that cover the capability today, or `none`. Those are the checks a rebuild keeps passing
  (selectors may change, assertions not).
- **Workstream:** the redesign workstream that owns the item: `Foundation`, `Shell`, `Conversation`, `Overlays`,
  `Calls & media`, `Windows`, `Auth & pages` or `Main process`. Appendix A gives the workstream of each file, with a note
  where a file didn't fit the split.
- **New home** and **Status:** empty for now. Each workstream fills in where the item lives in the new design, and its
  status once walked.

The appendices index the same items another way: A by file (every file `git ls-files` lists under `src/mainview/src`
and `src/bun`, with `app.html`, `app.css`, the configs and `src/mainview/static`), B by shortcut, C by menu, D by what
the app persists on the device, E by the messages between the webview and the Bun process.

The ledger has 1594 items in 94 sections.

| P-082 | Every API request carries the access token (Authorization: Bearer), the session token (X-Session-Token) and the API's cookies. | lib/api/api.ts: coreFetch | all | In dev tools' network panel, open any request to the API: both headers are there. | api.test.ts, test-preload.test.ts | Foundation |  |  |
| P-083 | The session is renewed before it runs out (24 h): as the app starts and when its window comes back into view if its tokens are an hour old or more, and every 10 minutes once they're 23 h old, so a computer that slept through the last hour doesn't sign you out. | lib/session.ts: renewalDue; lib/api/api.ts: keepSessionFresh; `routes/app/+page.svelte: onMount` (`keepSessionFresh`, `session.connected`, `createWebSocketConnection`, `onDestroy`); `lib/api/api.ts` | all | Sign in, set `duvet_session_refreshed_at` two hours back in localStorage, hide and show the window: a POST /api/auth/refresh goes out. | session.test.ts, session-start.test.ts | Foundation |  |  |
| P-084 | As the app starts, the gateway connection opens at once and the session's renewal follows once the app is up; only tokens in their last hour are renewed first, as the gateway would refuse them. | lib/session.ts: renewalFirst; lib/api/api.ts: keepSessionFresh | all | Set `duvet_session_refreshed_at` 2 h back and reload: the socket opens before the refresh. Set it 23.5 h back: the refresh goes first. | session.test.ts, session-start.test.ts | Foundation |  |  |
| P-085 | A request refused with 401 renews the session once and is sent again with the new tokens (a form's upload starting over); requests refused together share one renewal, and one sent before a renewal that finished meanwhile reuses its tokens. | lib/api/api.ts: coreFetch, refreshSession | all | Corrupt `duvet_session_token` in localStorage and open a room: one /api/auth/refresh, then the messages load. | api.test.ts | Foundation |  |  |

| P-087 | A rate-limited renewal (429) waits as long as the API says (Retry-After or X-RateLimit-Reset-After, 20 s at most each, up to 3 more tries) and doesn't sign you out. | lib/api/api.ts: requestRefresh | all | Hard to see live: needs an API that rate-limits /api/auth/refresh; the session stays signed in. | none | Foundation |  |  |
| P-088 | A renewal that gets no answer (a network error, or 20 s of silence) is tried once more 3 s later, inside the API's 30 s grace for a rotated session; still without an answer it fails without signing you out; an API error doesn't sign you out either. | lib/session.ts: renewOnceMore; lib/api/api.ts: requestRefresh | all | Go offline while a renewal is due: the app stays signed in and renews once back online. | session.test.ts, api.test.ts | Foundation |  |  |

## Appendix B, shortcuts

Every key binding: global shortcuts, keys handled inside a component, and modifiers on clicks. macOS uses ⌘ where the others use Ctrl unless a row says otherwise.

| Keys | Action | Defined in | Active when | P-item |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Enter` | Sign in | routes/(auth)/sign-in/+page.svelte: form onsubmit | Focus in Email or Password, not while signing in | P-001 |
| `Enter` | Create the account | routes/(auth)/sign-up/+page.svelte: form onsubmit | Focus in a blank of the sentence, once "Create my account" is enabled | P-021 |
| `Enter` | Send the reset email | routes/(auth)/forgot-password/+page.svelte: form onsubmit | Focus in Email, the email not empty | P-036 |
| `Enter` | Reset the password | routes/(auth)/reset-password/+page.svelte: form onsubmit | Focus in a password field, both valid and matching | P-042 |
| `Escape` | Close the Terms of use dialog | routes/(auth)/sign-up/+page.svelte: Dialog.Root (bits-ui) | The Terms of use dialog is open | P-029 |
| `Space` | Tick or untick "I agree to the Terms of use" | routes/(auth)/sign-up/+page.svelte: Checkbox.Root (bits-ui) | Focus on the terms checkbox | P-028 |
| `Tab` | Skips the sign-up sentence's lines not yet shown | routes/(auth)/sign-up/+page.svelte: inert | On the sign-up page | P-024 |
| `⌘K` (macOS) / `Ctrl+K` (Windows, Linux, web) | Open or close the quick switcher | `routes/app/+page.svelte: handleWindowKeydown` | The app has loaded; no Alt or Shift; no other dialog is open (unless it's the switcher itself) | P-211 |
| `Ctrl+W` (every platform, macOS too) | Close the focused pane's active tab | `lib/components/PaneView.svelte: handleCtrlW` | A pane is focused and has an active tab; works with focus anywhere, the message box included | P-177 |
| `Enter`, `Space` | Show the focused tab | `lib/components/TabBar.svelte` (tab `onkeydown`) | Keyboard focus is on a tab | P-186 |
| `⌘K` / `Ctrl+K` | Open the quick switcher (the column's search button shows the label) | `routes/app/+page.svelte` (label in `lib/components/SpaceNav.svelte: shortcutLabel`) | Anywhere, no other dialog open | P-221 |
| `f`, then a mark's letters | Jump to a row of the spaces column | `lib/components/KeyboardLayer.svelte` (targets marked `data-jump` in this area's files) | Keyboard navigation on, focus out of the message box | P-1397 |
| `ArrowDown` | Move the focus to the next room | `lib/components/ChannelsSidebar.svelte: onKeyDown` | Focus on a room in the room list (one the user may join) | P-271 |
| `ArrowUp` | Move the focus to the previous room | `lib/components/ChannelsSidebar.svelte: onKeyDown` | Focus on a room in the room list | P-271 |
| `Enter` or `Space` | Open the focused room | `lib/components/ChannelsSidebar.svelte: onKeyDown` | Focus on a room in the room list | P-271 |
| Double-click | Join a voice room's voice | `lib/components/ChannelsSidebar.svelte: ondblclick` | On a voice room the user may join | P-270 |
| `Enter` | Create the room | `lib/components/ChannelsSidebar.svelte: channelName onkeydown` | Focus in the Create Room dialog's name field | P-285 |
| `Escape` | Close the Create Room or Delete Room dialog | `lib/components/ChannelsSidebar.svelte: Dialog.Root` (bits-ui) | Either dialog open | P-287 |
| `Enter` | Send the friend request | `lib/components/FriendsView.svelte: form onsubmit` | Focus in the Add friend card's User ID field | P-341 |
| `Escape` | Nothing: the New group dialog's key handlers are empty, it closes only with Cancel or a click outside | `lib/components/FriendsView.svelte: onkeydown={() => {}}` | New group dialog open | P-304 |
| Triple-click | Start editing the message | `lib/components/MessageList.svelte` (text `onclick`) | On the text of one's own message | P-410 |
| `Enter` | Start editing the message | `lib/components/MessageList.svelte` (text `onkeydown`) | The text of one's own message has focus | P-410 |
| `Enter` | Save the edit | `lib/components/ChatInput.svelte` (`onSubmit` from `MessageList.svelte: saveEdit`) | Inline editor open | P-411 |
| `Escape` | Cancel the edit | `lib/components/ChatInput.svelte` (`onCancel` from `MessageList.svelte: cancelEdit`) | Inline editor open | P-411 |
| `r` | Reply to the message (hint shown on the row) | `lib/keyboard.svelte.ts` (hint in `MessageList.svelte`) | Keyboard navigation cursor on a message | P-1386 |
| `e` | Edit the message (hint shown on the row) | `lib/keyboard.svelte.ts` (hint in `MessageList.svelte`) | Keyboard navigation cursor on one's own message | P-1386 |
| Right-click | Open the user menu | `lib/components/MessageList.svelte: openUserMenu` | On an author's avatar (not a webhook's) | P-415 |
| Right-click | Open who reacted, on that emoji | `lib/components/MessageReactions.svelte` | On a reaction chip | P-480 |
| `Escape` | Close the user menu | `lib/components/MessageList.svelte` (`svelte:window` keydown) | User menu open | P-420 |
| `Escape` | Close the thread dialog | `lib/components/MessageList.svelte` (bits-ui `Dialog`) | Thread dialog open | P-403 |
| `Escape` | Close the Flags & Badges dialog | `lib/components/MessageList.svelte` (bits-ui `Dialog`) | Staff dialog open | P-419 |
| `Escape` | Close a bot's select menu (sends a changed multi-choice) | `lib/components/ComponentSelect.svelte` (bits-ui `Popover`) | Select menu open | P-495 |
| `Escape` | Close the image preview | `lib/components/ImagePreview.svelte: handleKeyDown` | Image preview open | P-463 |
| `+` or `=` | Zoom in | `lib/components/ImagePreview.svelte: handleKeyDown` | Image preview open | P-459 |
| `-` | Zoom out | `lib/components/ImagePreview.svelte: handleKeyDown` | Image preview open | P-459 |
| Mouse wheel | Zoom in or out | `lib/components/ImagePreview.svelte: handleWheel` | Image preview open | P-459 |
| `0` | Reset zoom, rotation and position | `lib/components/ImagePreview.svelte: handleKeyDown` | Image preview open | P-462 |
| `]` or `ArrowRight` | Rotate 10° right | `lib/components/ImagePreview.svelte: handleKeyDown` | Image preview open | P-460 |
| `[` or `ArrowLeft` | Rotate 10° left | `lib/components/ImagePreview.svelte: handleKeyDown` | Image preview open | P-460 |
| `Ctrl+Shift`+wheel | Rotate 10° per notch | `lib/components/ImagePreview.svelte: handleWheel` | Image preview open | P-460 |
| Drag (left button) | Pan the image | `lib/components/ImagePreview.svelte: handlePointerDown` | Image preview open | P-461 |
| `Enter` | Send the message | `lib/components/ChatInput.svelte: handleKeyDown` | Focus in a message box, no suggestion list open, no IME composing | P-561 |
| `Shift+Enter` | New line | `lib/components/ChatInput.svelte: handleKeyDown` | Focus in a message box | P-561 |
| `Enter` | Wait for the commands list, then pick the first match or send | `lib/components/ChatInput.svelte: handleKeyDown` | The box holds a command's name and the Space's commands haven't come yet | P-608 |
| `↑` | Edit the user's newest own message | `lib/components/ChatInput.svelte: handleKeyDown`; `lib/components/Chat.svelte: tryEditLastMessage` | Message box empty, no Ctrl, no suggestion list | P-526 |

## Appendix D, what the app persists

Everything the app keeps on the device: a preference here that silently resets after the redesign is a lost feature.

| Key | Storage | Holds | Read and written by | P-item |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `duvet_session_token` | localStorage | The access token (Bearer) | lib/stores/auth.ts: accessToken; lib/stores.svelte.ts: userSession; lib/api/auth.ts: logIn, logout; lib/api/api.ts: coreFetch, requestRefresh; app.html (redirects, early socket); also read by lib/api/ws/connection.ts | P-081, P-082, P-067, P-068, P-086, P-1000, P-1456, P-1461, P-1464, P-1465 |
| `duvet_refresh_token` | localStorage | The session token, sent as X-Session-Token and used to renew | lib/api/auth.ts: logIn, logout; lib/api/api.ts: coreFetch, requestRefresh, keepSessionFresh | P-081, P-082, P-083, P-085, P-1000 |
| `duvet_session_refreshed_at` | localStorage | When the tokens were last issued (ms since 1970) | lib/api/api.ts: markSessionRefreshed, keepSessionFresh; lib/api/auth.ts: logIn; app.html (early socket) | P-083, P-084, P-1456 |
| `duvet.start.v1` | localStorage | JSON `{ gateway, api }`: the addresses the app last used | Read by app.html; written by lib/api/ws/connection.ts (another area) | P-1456 |
| `duvet.theme.v1` | localStorage | The theme picked in Appearance: "light", "dark", or System when absent | Read by app.html; written by lib/theme.ts (another area) | P-099, P-1055 |
| `duvet_noise_suppression` | localStorage | "true" or "false" | lib/stores.svelte.ts: noiseSuppression; toggled from lib/components/UserStatusBar.svelte | P-842, P-968 |
| `duvet_voice_faces` | localStorage | "true" or "false" | lib/stores.svelte.ts: voiceFaces; toggled from lib/components/UserSettings.svelte | P-1064 |
| `duvet_share_spotify` | localStorage | "true" or "false" | lib/stores.svelte.ts: shareSpotify; toggled from lib/components/UserSettings.svelte | P-1067, P-1066 |
| `duvet_mic_id` | localStorage | The picked microphone's device ID (written only when not empty) | lib/stores.svelte.ts: selectedMicId; set from lib/components/UserStatusBar.svelte | P-843, P-846 |
| `duvet_camera_id` | localStorage | The picked camera's device ID (written only when not empty) | lib/stores.svelte.ts: selectedCameraId; set from lib/components/VoiceChannelView.svelte | P-888, P-889 |
| `duvet_session_token` | cookie (HttpOnly, set by the API at login, 30 days) | The session token | Sent with every request by lib/api/api.ts (credentials include), the renewal included; never read by the page | P-082 |
| `duvet.openRooms.v1` | localStorage | JSON `{ pane, activeSpaceId }`: the pane tree (leaves with their tabs and active tab, splits with direction and ratio) and the Space selected in the column | `routes/app/+page.svelte` (restore once at start, save on every change; removed when unreadable) | P-129, P-130, P-131 |
| `duvet.layout.panels.v1` | localStorage | JSON `{ membersColumn, showMembersSidebar, showChannelsSidebar, spacesColumn, openChats, keyboardNavigation }` (`showMembersSidebar` kept for older builds) | `lib/layout-prefs.svelte.ts` (read at start, written on every change), parsed by `lib/layout-prefs.ts: parseLayoutPrefs`; changed from `routes/app/+page.svelte`, `lib/components/PaneView.svelte` and `lib/components/UserSettings.svelte` | P-151, P-152, P-156, P-157, P-158, P-159, P-160, P-1384, P-162, P-163, P-714, P-1056, P-1057, P-1058 |
| `duvet.nav.pinned.v1` | localStorage | JSON array of the pinned Space IDs, in pin order | `lib/nav-prefs.svelte.ts` (`togglePinned` writes; read at start via `lib/nav.ts: parsePinned`); used by SpaceNav and SpaceEdge | P-228, P-225, P-255 |
| `duvet.nav.now.v1` | localStorage | JSON `{ roomsFolded, friendsFolded, allRooms }` for the Now section | `lib/nav-prefs.svelte.ts` (`toggleNow` writes; read at start via `lib/nav.ts: parseNowPrefs`); used by NowSection | P-315, P-316, P-320 |
| `duvet.code_theme_url` | localStorage | URL of a custom code highlighting theme (JSON or JSONC) | Read by `lib/markdown.ts: getThemeUrl` (as the highlighter is made); written by `loadCustomTheme`, removed by `resetTheme` (called from Appearance settings) | P-1060, P-1062 |
| `duvet_audio_settings` | localStorage | JSON of sound volumes, 0 to 200 each (default 100): `master`, `notification`, `join_vc`, `leave_vc`, `voice_calling`, `screen_sharing_on`, `screen_sharing_off` | `lib/stores/audio.svelte.ts: load, save` (written through `set` and `setMaster`, called from `UserSettings`' sounds section) | P-1050, P-1051, P-1054, P-1431, P-1432 |
| `duvet_audio_volume` | localStorage | Voice message and audio file playback volume, 0 to 1 (default 1) | `lib/stores/voiceNotePlayer.svelte.ts: audioVolume` (slider in `VoiceNoteBar` and `AudioFilePlayer`) | P-984 |
| `duvet-notifications` | BroadcastChannel and Web Lock name (not storage) | Tabs' focus messages (hello, focus, bye) and the lock held by the tab that notifies | `lib/tab-notifications.ts: TabCoordinator` | P-1446 |
| `startup.log` | file: `<userData>/startup.log`, where userData is `~/Library/Application Support/com.duvet.desktop/<channel>` (macOS), `%LOCALAPPDATA%\com.duvet.desktop\<channel>` (Windows), `$XDG_DATA_HOME` or `~/.local/share` + `/com.duvet.desktop/<channel>` (Linux) | One line per start, each step in ms since Bun started; the last 50 starts | Written by `src/bun/startup.ts: writeStartupLine` from `src/bun/index.ts: startupTimings`; read by people | P-1545 |
| `notification-avatars/<sha256 of photo URL>.png` or `.jpg` | file: `%LOCALAPPDATA%\com.duvet.desktop\<channel>\notification-avatars\` (userCache) | Authors' photos for toasts as small PNG or JPEG; the newest 200 | `src/bun/notification-avatars.ts: avatarFile, keepNewest`; read by Windows when it draws a toast | P-1563, P-1564 |
| `notification-icon.png` | file: `%LOCALAPPDATA%\com.duvet.desktop\<channel>\notification-icon.png` | Duvet's 256 px icon for the toast sender, rewritten at every start | Written by `src/bun/notification-setup.ts: setUpNotifications`; read by Windows through `IconUri` | P-1570 |
| `notification-forwarder.js` | file: `%LOCALAPPDATA%\com.duvet.desktop\<channel>\notification-forwarder.js` | The script Windows runs for a toast click, rewritten at every start | Written by `src/bun/notification-setup.ts: setUpNotifications` with `src/bun/notification-forwarder.ts: forwarderScript`; run by the scheme's handler | P-1567, P-1568 |
| `HKCU\Software\Classes\AppUserModelId\<aumid>` (`DisplayName`, `IconUri`) | registry (Windows, user hive) | The toast sender's name ("Duvet", "Duvet Canary", "Duvet Dev") and icon path | Written by `src/bun/notification-setup.ts: registryCommands` at every start; read by Windows | P-1570 |
| `HKCU\Software\Classes\<scheme>` (default `URL:<name>`, `URL Protocol`, `shell\open\command`) | registry (Windows, user hive) | The channel's link scheme (`duvet`, `duvet-canary`, `duvet-dev`) and its handler: `conhost.exe --headless "<bun.exe>" "<forwarder>" "%1"` | Written by `src/bun/notification-setup.ts: registryCommands`; run by Windows on a toast click | P-1567, P-1571 |
| `duvet-ipc.sock` | file (Unix socket): in `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR`, else `$TMPDIR`, `$TMP`, `$TEMP` or `/tmp` | The endpoint other apps send activities to; a stale one is deleted at start | `src/bun/ipc.ts: ipcPath`; `src/bun/index.ts: Bun.listen, unlinkSync` | P-1583, P-1593 |
| `duvet-window-snap-<pid>.c` | file: the system temp folder, deleted right after it compiles | `window-snap.c`'s source for TinyCC | `src/bun/window-snap.ts: compile` | P-1530 |

## Appendix E, the bridge

Every RPC request and message between the webview (`lib/electrobun-rpc.ts`) and the Bun process (`src/bun/index.ts`). All of them are desktop-only.

| Name | Kind | Direction | Purpose | P-item |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `startupTimings` | message | webview→bun | The start's step times, which Bun writes to startup.log; sent through reportStartup's callback from routes/app/+page.svelte | P-100, P-1545 |
| `setSpotifyActivity` | message | webview→bun | Turns sharing what Spotify plays on or off. Sent by `lib/api/ws/connection.ts` whenever the Activity Privacy switch (`shareSpotify`) changes; handled in `src/bun/index.ts` | P-1066, P-1580, P-1592 |
| `showNotification` | request | webview→bun | Show a Windows toast for a message (author, text, place, avatar, link, silent); answers whether it showed | P-1560, P-1441, P-1563, P-1566 |
| `takeClickedMessage` | request | webview→bun | Take the message of the toast clicked last, once | P-1567, P-1568, P-1569 |
| `messageClicked` | message | bun→webview | A toast was clicked: the page takes its message | P-1567 |
| `activityUpdate` | message | bun→webview | The user's activities (programs, Spotify) as the Bun process reads them | P-1466, P-1066, P-1583, P-1585 |
| `nativeWindowDrag` | request | webview→bun | Whether Windows' move loop drags the window; true makes the title bar report its presses | P-1528, P-1533 |
| `closeWindow` | message | webview→bun | Close the window, which quits the app | P-1526 |
| `minimizeWindow` | message | webview→bun | Minimize the window | P-217 |
| `maximizeWindow` | message | webview→bun | Maximize or restore (Windows' own on Windows, Duvet's Maximizer elsewhere) | P-1520, P-1524, P-220 |
| `startWindowDrag` | message | webview→bun | A primary press on the title bar on Windows: Bun follows it and starts the move loop once it's a drag | P-1528 |
| `openExternal` | message | webview→bun | Open an http(s) or mailto URL in the system browser or mail client | P-1548, P-1549, P-1551 |
| `setWindowFrame` | message | webview→bun | Declared in Bun's schema only: no handler, and the webview never sends it | none |
| `startWindowResize` | message | webview→bun | Declared in Bun's schema only: no handler, and the webview never sends it | none |
| `stopWindowResize` | message | webview→bun | Declared in Bun's schema only: no handler, and the webview never sends it | none |
| `activityImage` | request | bun→webview | Make fetched image bytes (base64) a small inline `data:image/` URI with the page's canvas, or null | P-1587, P-1563 |
| `new-window-open` | message (Electrobun webview event) | webview→bun | A Ctrl or ⌘ click on a link, from Electrobun's preload; Bun opens http(s) and mailto in the system browser | P-1550 |
| `dom-ready` | message (Electrobun webview event) | webview→bun | A page loaded: Bun sets the navigation rules | P-1555 |
| `startWindowMove` / `stopWindowMove` | message (Electrobun internal) | webview→bun | A press on the title bar's drag region (`electrobun-webkit-app-region-drag`) | P-1527 |
| `SET_ACTIVITY` | message (IPC socket line) | external app→bun | An app's activity, fitted and shown | P-1583, P-1586 |
| `CLEAR_ACTIVITY` | message (IPC socket line) | external app→bun | An app clears its activity | P-1584 |
| `OPEN_URL` | message (notification pipe line, `\\.\pipe\duvet-open-<channel>`) | forwarder→bun | A toast click's link, handed to the running Duvet | P-1567, P-1568 |
